台玻(1802 TT) Buy

破曉巨浪已然凝聚。 
投資建議：高階玻纖布 Low Dk2/Low CTE出貨動能攀升，帶動
3Q26 毛利率季增逾 8%， 10/2026 營收創高。研究部同步上修
4Q26、 2027 年獲利預估。預估台玻 2027 年稅後 EPS 4.84 元。
給予投資建議 Buy，目標價 100 元 (2027 年 PBR3.97X)。  
3Q26 毛利率再創高至 31.60%：台玻 3Q26 受惠於 Low Dk2 、
Low CTE 出貨比重拉升，3Q26 毛利率較 2Q26 提升 8.27%至
31.60%，亦優於原預期之 26.23%。 3Q26 稅後純益 27.05 億元、
稅後 EPS 0.93 元，均優於預期。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.04%、 YoY+56.49%：台玻 10/2026 營收
55.66 億元，MoM+12.04%、YoY+56.49%，優於預期，主因玻纖
布事業營收持續攀升，整體出貨動能較 09/2026 成長。以研究
部預期當前玻纖布營收比重約 40~46%，高階玻纖布占其中
40~42%推估，10/2026 高階玻纖布營收貢獻 MoM+13~16%。  
預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 比重仍高於 Low CTE：台玻高階玻纖布
以 Low Dk2/Low CTE 為主要營收來源。Low CTE 已於 2025 年
底開始小量出貨，目前產品出貨量已較 2026 年初顯著增加，預
期營收佔比將於 2027 年持續提升，並於 2H27~2028 年新產能
開出後，追趕 Low Dk2 。預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營收比重仍將
高於 Low Dk1 及 Low CTE。  
積極擴建 Low Dk/Low CTE產能，期待擴產效益顯現，預估 2027
年稅後 EPS 4.84 元：11/02/2026 董事會通過桃園廠、鹿港廠窯
爐增建及子公司台嘉蚌埠玻璃纖維有限公司冷修擴充案，預估
投入金額約新台幣 16.3 億元及人民幣 11.5 億元。Low CTE 產
品已取得國內載板大廠認證，2028 年訂單洽談順暢。研究部預
估台玻 2027 年營收 711.51 億元，YoY+33.24%，稅後純益 140.68
億元，YoY+69.11%，稅後 EPS 4.84 元。

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

2 群益投顧 
  台玻 ESG： 
ESG 方面，台玻堅守四大主軸，包括強化永續環境、落實誠信經營、關懷社
會公益及發展友善職場。強化永續環境方面，台玻在符合法規條件下，積極
減降生產過程對環境的外部成本，並妥善安置生產後產生之廢棄物；落實誠
信經營方面，台玻始終秉持該核心經營理念，與對股東、員工及利害關係人
之承諾及責任；關懷社會公益方面，透過參與社會公益活動，落實回饋社會
及關懷責任，並提供員工及經銷商子女相關獎學金，培育人才；發展友善職
場方面，尊重人權及性別平等，提供員工舒適且安全職場環境。 
公司簡介： 
台玻成立於 1964 年，事業群分別平板事業部、纖維事業部、容器事業部、
汽車玻璃事業部。主要產品包括平板玻璃、玻璃纖維及玻璃器皿，生產基地
位於台灣及中國兩地。平板玻璃產品均已通過歐盟 CE、美國 ANSI、台灣
CNS 相關規範，低輻射 Low-E 複層玻能維持可見光情況下，阻擋紅外線熱
能進入室內，具良好隔熱效果，主要用於建築物玻璃帷幕牆。公司也有發展
玻璃基板，有望搭上 CoPoS 題材。玻璃纖維方面，台玻有複合材料用的 FRP
及玻璃纖維布。FRP 玻璃纖維具備優異耐腐蝕耐候性、低成本及高強度特
性。玻璃纖維布廣泛運用於 5G 伺服器、AI 伺服器、手機平板及筆電等產
品。 
圖一、台玻產品組合(營收比重)

資料來源：台玻，群益投顧預估彙整 
台玻 2026 年高階玻纖布市占率上看 45%， 2028 年訂單洽談順暢： 
台玻從 1990 年開始生產玻纖布，技術並非一蹴可磯，而是多年人才培育及
技術經驗累積推敲而得。最開始是與美國 Owens Corning 簽署技術合作授
權，從中取得全球最先進玻璃纖維漿料配方及技術指導，並向日本 Kanebo
購入織布技術。台玻亦為繼 Nittobo、 Ibiden 後，第三家廠商具備自行研發
量產 Low Dk 玻璃纖維布廠商，並已通過國內外大廠認證，於 2025 年陸續
出貨。主要應用於 5G 基礎建設、高階伺服器、高階顯卡及車用等領域。 
AI 投資強勁推升載板加速升級，Low Dk1、 Low Dk2 玻纖布及 Low CTE 玻
纖布需求隨之暴漲。現階段台玻產能已達滿載，且訂單能見度至 2027 年，
2028 年訂單洽談順暢。公司表示，2026 年目標高階玻纖布市占率 40%~45%。

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

3 群益投顧 
台玻客戶需求達 2028 年，Nittobo 指出 2029 年度產能擴增至 2025 年度之
3.6 倍： 
台玻 T-glass 已通過國內外廠商認證，Ibiden 亦表示 T-glass 仍處於供不應
求階段。T-glass 尚待日本大廠 Nittobo 於 2H27 後開出新產能，才得以部分
緩解，且考量技術門檻偏高、窯爐建置期長，產能放量亦需時間等待。台玻
董事長更於近日指出，客戶需求已達 2028 年，當前公司供給仍追不上需求。
Nittobo 亦再次表示 T-glass 需求強勁。Nittobo 目前產線均處滿載生產階段，
且決定於台灣工廠增建玻纖絲製造熔爐，並於日本福島增設玻纖布生產工
廠。雖 Nittobo 於 2028 年度產能將為 2025 年度之 3 倍，惟觀察產品需求已
非當前擴產速度得以消化。Nittobo 於 11/05/2026 財報說明會上表示，將投
資 200 億日元，擴充日本福島縣與台灣營運據點之生產設備，增產 T-glass，
目標 2029 年度產能擴增至 2025 年度之 3.6 倍。 
產品報價方面，富喬於 06/26/2026 調漲 E 布報價 30%。大陸 CCL 龍頭廠建
滔積層板 04/28/2026 發布漲價通知，宣布即日起 FR-4 銅箔基板、半固化片
（ PP）將再調漲 10%。 05/27/2026 再調漲板料報價 10%、 PP 調漲 20%。
07/06/2026 分別再次調漲 FR-4、CEM-1/22F 及 PP 等產品報價約 15%、10%、
15%。08/28/2026 分別調漲 FR-4、7628(含 )以上厚布、7628 以下薄布約 10%、
10%、 20%。為因應市場強勁需求，富喬、建滔積層板 08/2026 後仍持續調
漲產品報價。 
3Q26 毛利率再創高，QoQ+8.27%至 31.60%：  
台玻 3Q26 受惠於 Low Dk2 、 Low CTE 等高毛利產品出貨比重拉升，帶動
3Q26 營業毛利 QoQ+58.80%、 YoY+257.07%，且毛利率較 2Q26 提升 8.27%
至 31.60%，亦優於原預期之 26.23%。因此 3Q26 稅後純益 27.05 億元、稅
後 EPS 0.93 元，均優於原預期之 20.47 億元、0.70 元。1~3Q26 台玻累計營
收達 364.41 億元，YoY+18.69%，毛利率 25.38%，稅後純益 45.66 億元，
YoY 由虧轉盈，稅後 EPS 1.57 元。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.04%、 YoY+56.49%：  
台玻 10/2026 營收 55.66 億元，MoM+12.04%、 YoY+56.49%，優於預期，與
原預期值 52.21 億元，差異約+6.2%。主因玻纖布事業營收持續攀升，整體
出貨動能較 09/2026 成長，部分抵銷中國區域平板玻璃尚未損平影響。以研
究部預期當前玻纖布營收比重約 40~46%，高階玻纖布占其中 40~42%推估，
10/2026 高階玻纖布營收貢獻約 MoM+13~16%。  
不只有 Low Dk/Low CTE， Q 布也為台玻戰略儲備： 
2027~2028 年伴隨 AI 伺服器升級及機櫃出貨增加下，CCL 板材有望大量導
入 M9 等高階材料，並搭配 Q 布或 Low Dk2 等混合配方，且台玻亦具 Q 布
接單出貨能力。 
日前台玻參展 TPCA Show，展出 Q 布玻纖紗，顯示其 Q 布技術儲備，大秀
高階肌肉。惟觀察 AI 伺服器規格調整，預期 2027 年產品需求仍以 Low Dk
系列玻纖布為主，大量搭載 Q 布尚早。不同材料規格之玻纖布，其製程方
式雖雷同，然配方均有差異，窯爐使用亦不同，故產能簽訂後，供應鏈替換
廠商可能性不高。預期台玻將視下游廠商產品需求，決議 Q 布生產時程。

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 比重仍高於 Low CTE：  
台玻 E-glass 轉至中國產銷，當前高階玻纖布以 Low Dk2/Low CTE 為主要
營收來源。Low CTE 已於 2025 年底開始小量出貨，目前產品出貨量已較
2026 年初顯著增加，預期營收佔比將於 2027 年持續提升，並於 2H27~2028
年新產能開出後，追趕 Low Dk2。目前下游終端需求仍以 Low Dk 產品為主
力，預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營收比重仍將高於 Low Dk1 及 Low CTE。  
積極擴建 Low Dk/Low CTE 產能，期待擴產效益顯現，預估 2027 年稅後
EPS 4.84 元： 
產能布局方面，05/11/2026 經董事會宣布，為因應市場對於高階玻纖布需求
上揚，新增資本支出 22.42 億元，用以擴建公司桃園廠及鹿港廠窯爐及冷修
案，預計主要用於增加 Low Dk2、 T-glass 產能、提高市佔率，最快 2Q27 投
產。06/12/2026 股東會董事長指出，2026 年預計再增加 20 億元資本支出，
用於擴充 Low Dk(低介電損耗)、Low CTE(低熱膨脹係數)之 T 布產能，並表
示產品供不應求將延續至 2027 年底。目前高階玻纖布擴產計畫共分三期，
第一期已於 2026 年投入並量產，第二期預計 2026 年完成，新增之 20 億元
為第三期投資計畫，預計 2027 年完工。11/02/2026 董事會通過桃園廠、鹿
港廠窯爐增建及子公司台嘉蚌埠玻璃纖維有限公司冷修擴充案，預估投入
金額約新台幣 16.3 億元及人民幣 11.5 億元。 
產品報價方面，台玻始終秉持於維繫良好客戶關係，並以客戶需求為營運宗
旨。雖 2025~2026 年以來 E-glass 報價倍數增長，惟基於多年來與客戶合作
建立之信任及當前產品利潤貢獻尚屬可控範圍，故而預期將不急於大幅反
映產品報價，但亦不排除部分調整產品價格。 
預期 2027 年 Low Dk2/Low CTE玻纖布出貨量仍將較 2026 年提升，Low Dk1
營收貢獻亦將延續動能。台玻 Low CTE 產品已取得國內載板大廠認證，訂
單能見度已達 2027 年，且 2028 年訂單洽談順暢。若供應鏈產品需求無較大
質變，2028 年玻纖布事業群仍將維持成長格局。預估台玻 4Q26 底 ~2027 年
玻纖布營收比重可望超越平板玻璃及玻璃器皿。研究部預估台玻 2026 年營
收 534.01 億元，YoY+28.70%，毛利率 28.28%，稅後純益 83.19 億元，YoY
虧轉盈，稅後 EPS 2.86 元；2027 年營收 711.51 億元，YoY+33.24%，毛利
率 34.43%，稅後純益 140.68 億元，YoY+69.11%，稅後 EPS 4.84 元。 
圖二、台玻季營收變化預估

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 035: 2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更 
～銀行金利市場化の長い道のり～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹 
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 人民銀がリバースレポ翌日物の日常オペを開始。短期金利の誘導精度を高める狙い 
中国の中央銀行に当たる中国人民銀行（PBOC）が10月30日、市場に短期流動性を供給する「売り戻
し条件付き債券購入（リバースレポ）」翌日物の公開市場操作（オペ）を11月2日より毎営業日実施す
ると発表した。これまで原則として毎営業日実施していたリバースレポ7日物から切り替える。翌日物
のオペは固定金利、数量入札の方式で行い、その金利を政策金利と位置づける。インターバンク市場
のレポ取引では翌日物が大半を占めていることから、政策金利を7日物から翌日物に変更することで
期間のズレを解消し、短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙いがある。新たなオペ初日の金利は1.25％
であった（前営業日のリバースレポ7日物の金利は1.40％）。 
この動きは、PBOCの潘功勝行長（総裁）が10月19日の講演で明らかにしていた金融政策運営の改革
方針を実行に移したものである。潘氏は「銀行間債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）は事実上、中
央銀行の金融政策が調節する目標金利となっているが、それと同時に、中央銀行の公開市場操作の主
要な種類と主要な政策金利の期限はなお7日で、両者は完全には一致しない」ことを認めた上で、「適
切なタイミングで公開市場の主要な操作種類の期限を翌日物に切り替え、公開市場の翌日物レポ金利
を主要な政策金利とすることを検討している」と予告していた。この切り替えによって、PBOCの金融
政策運営は米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）や日本銀行のそれに近づくことになる。 
さらに、潘氏は銀行貸出金利の改革を進めていることも明らかにした。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）を
参照して貸出金利を決定するこれまでの運用に加え、DR001などの短期市場金利を直接参照して金利
を決定する方法を試験的に導入したのである。今後、財務力のある企業は商業銀行と協議の上でDR001
もしくはLPRを参照した貸出金利を選択することができ、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを参照
した金利を適用するとしている。経済誌『財新』によれば、2026年7月以降にDR001を参照した銀行貸
出がすでに全国で100件以上実施されているという。 
PBOCが新たな銀行貸出金利の決定メカニズムを模索する背景には、LPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、
貸出金利の実勢を十分に反映できていないことがあるとみられる。これは、2013年7月に下限規制が撤
廃される前の貸出基準金利が基準の70％まで引き下げを許容していた歴史的経緯から、信用力の高い
顧客にLPR以下の金利で貸出を実行する慣行が残っているためでもある。潘氏は改革を進めると同時
に、すでに実務的な役割を終えている貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を正式に廃止するとした。 
2026 年 11 月 9 日

### Page 2

2

■ 貸出基準金利から最優遇貸出金利へ移行。ただ、実勢金利は「最優遇」から乖離 
PBOCは2010年代以降、銀行金利の市場化改革を慎重に推し進めてきた（図表1）。改革以前はPBOC
が貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を直接コントロールし、商業銀行がその上下一定の範囲内で貸出・預
金金利を決定する制度となっていたが、PBOCは2013年7月に貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃し、同年10月
には全国銀行間コール市場センター（全国銀行間同業拆借中心。以下「コール市場センター」）を通
じて貸出基礎金利（旧LPR）の発表を開始した。旧LPRは、主要9行（のち10行）の報告銀行がコール
市場センターに貸出の最優遇金利を報告し、センター側がそのうち最高値と最低値を除いた報告金利
を報告銀行の貸出残高で加重平均して算出するものであった。 
PBOCは銀行貸出金利が市場原理に基づき決定されるメカニズムへの移行を意図していたが、PBOC
が引き続き基準金利を操作して利下げを行い、報告銀行も基準金利を強く意識して最優遇金利を報告
したため、旧LPRは貸出基準金利に張り付いて変動した。PBOCは「銀行が貸出を実行する際、多くが
なお貸出基準金利を参照して金利を決定し、特に一部の銀行が協調行動を通じて貸出基準金利の一定
図表1 銀行金利の市場化改革 
年月 No. 改革内容 
2004/10  銀行貸出金利の上限規制と銀行預金金利の下限規制を撤廃 
2013/7  ① 銀行貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の下限は貸出基準金利の 70％） 
2013/10 ② 貸出基礎金利（旧 LPR）の発表を開始 
2015/10 ③ 銀行預金金利の上限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の上限は預金基準金利の 150％） 
2019/8 ④ 貸出市場報告金利（新 LPR）の発表を開始。中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）金利を参照金利に採用 
2024/7 [1] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを開始（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の▲0.2％と＋0.5％） 
 ⑤ リバースレポ 7 日物金利を LPR の参照金利に位置づけ 
2026/6 [2] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを変更（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の±0.25％） 
2026/11 ⑥ リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを開始。その金利を政策金利に位置づけ

（出所） 中国人民銀行、月岡（2015）（2013）より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

倍率（例えば0.9倍）を隠れた下限金利として設定しており、市場金利が実体経済に伝達することを妨
げている」と指摘している。 
そこで、PBOCは2019年8月、貸出市場報告金利（新LPR）と呼ばれる新たなメカニズムを導入する。
中長期流動性の供給ツールである中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）の金利を参照し、銀行がこれに資金
調達コストや市場需給、リスクプレミアムを反映した最優遇金利をコール市場センターに報告する方
法である。報告銀行は中小銀行を追加して18行（現在20行）まで拡大し、算出方法も加重平均から単
純平均に変更したことで、算出結果がウェイトの高い大手行の報告金利に引っ張られる傾向を排し、
指標としての包括性も高まった。この改革により、LPRは政策金利であるMLF1年物の金利と連動する
ようになったが、LPRが「最優遇」金利から乖離した状態は依然として解消されなかった。報告銀行
が旧LPRの時と同様にMLF金利を強く意識して金利を報告したためと考えられる。 
図表3は新LPRと実際の銀行貸出金利との関係を見たものである。LPRを下回る金利での貸出比率が
年々高まっていることが分かる。LPR1年物は2019年8月～2026年10月までの間に計▲1.25％Pt引き下げ
られているが、実勢金利は資金需要の低迷もあってそれ以上に下がっていることを示唆している。 
一方、銀行預金金利は2015年に上限規制を撤廃した後も、銀行間の過度な預金獲得競争を防ぐため
窓口指導や業界の自主規制の形をとった上限管理を維持し、2022年4月になって10年物国債利回りや
LPR1年物を参照して金利を決定するメカニズムを導入した。上限管理が廃止されたわけではないが、
預金金利は近年、市場金利の低下に伴って低下傾向にある。 
■ 市場化改革の成果を銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着させられるかが今後の焦点に 
PBOCは、2019年頃までに政策金利をリバースレポ7日物金利（短期）と MLF（中長期）の 2本立てと
する金融政策運営の枠組みを整え、新LPRの導入でその枠組みを明確化した（月岡（2024）参照）。
2024年7月には、政策金利をリバースレポ7日物に一本化し、LPRの参照金利もMLFからこれに移行さ
せた。中長期金利を市場の決定に委ねる形へと歩を進めたのである。 
PBOCは同時に、誘導目標とする短期金利を一定の範囲内で安定させるため金利コリドー（回廊）の

仕組みも整える（図表4）。2024年7月、流動性を吸収するレポと供給するリバースレポの翌日物の臨
時オペを開始し、レポ金利をリバースレポ7日物の▲0.2％Pt、リバースレポ金利を7日物の＋0.5％Ptに
設定することでコリドーの幅を狭めた。さらに、2026年6月には臨時オペの金利をそれぞれ▲0.25％Pt、
＋0.25％Ptに調整し、誘導目標とするDR001が臨時オペ金利の上下限を継続的に逸脱した場合にオペを
実施すると明確化した。リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを実施する準備を整えた形である。 
このように、PBOCは政策金利による短期市場金利の誘導精度を高めるとともに、市場金利の変化を
実際の銀行貸出金利へと円滑に波及させるメカニズムの構築を進めてきた。今回、政策金利をリバー
スレポ翌日物金利に変更し、LPRを介さずにDR001を直接参照する貸出金利の決定方式を導入したこ
とは、一連の市場化改革における重要な節目と位置づけることができる。DR001参照型の金利は指標
金利としてのLPRを代替するものではなく、PBOCは両者を併存させながら金利波及経路を改善するア
プローチを続けるとみられる。今後は、市場化改革の成果を商業銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着さ
せられるかが焦点となろう。

［参考文献］ 
月岡直樹（2024）「 中国が新たな金融政策の枠組み示唆 ～国債売買を政策ツールに活用も、量的緩和
は否定～」Mizuho RT EXPRESS（7月4日） 
月岡直樹（2015）「 中国人民銀行、預金金利を自由化へ 今年5度目の利下げを実施」みずほ中国ビジ
ネス・エクスプレス（10月26日）

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

ZDT 3Q26 core business (OPI) was 10%/11% higher than GSe and 
BBG consensus, mainly thanks to the better than expected product 
mix (high margin & high growth sectors accounted for 27% of total 
revenue in 3Q26 vs. our estimate only at 20-25%). In 3Q26, ZDT GM 
was 1.8/2.6ppt higher than GSe/BBG consensus, and was 27.7% up 
4.6ppt QoQ and 5.7ppt YoY, while the annual growth was only 
driven by the better product mix/ new business contribution 
(ABF/optical module/AI PCB), even considering these new 
businesses only accounted for 27% of total revenue.  
OPEX in 3Q26 increased 24% QoQ and 56% YoY due mainly to the 
higher investment in the R&D projects for future new high-end AI 
PCB/ optical transceiver / substrates business, which was the main 
reason for the company to see a 0.7/1.4ppt higher than GSe/BBG 
consensus OPEX to sales ratio in 3Q26.  
In 4Q26, we expect ZDT revenue to go up by ~29% QoQ, mainly due 
to the solid growth from the optical transceiver, substrates, and AI 
PCB businesses, with more new AI projects to ramp in 4Q26. 
Moreover, we expect the company’s GM to further expand to ~30% 
(2.2ppt QoQ or 7.3ppt YoY) thanks to a better product mix (we 
expect high growth/high margin new business will account for 
30-35% of the company’s total revenue in 4Q26, whose average GM 
should be at least 40-45% in 4Q26).  
Investment view 
We remain positive on ZDT’s AI business outlook, and expect the 
acceleration on ZDT’s AI PCB/Substrates business revenue to further 
drive the company’s overall GM level in coming years (we expect the 
two businesses’ average GM could surpass 50% in 2027, thanks to 
the favorable supply demand condition) while we also expect the 
high GM optical transceiver (50%+ GM now) will also see at least 
120% YoY growth in 2027, suggesting a significant GM expansion 
trend for the overall company in coming quarters/years.

### Page 3

We believe ZDT will continue to be the key share gainer in the AI PCB market. For AI GPU 
projects, we are seeing ZDT share increase from <5% in 2025 to ~10-15% in 2026, 
which, we believe, will further go up to 30%+ in long term (2028 and beyond), thanks to 
the company’s proactive investment in the R&D on the latest PCB technology, as well as 
the company’s solid investment in the AI grade PCB capacity. For AI ASIC projects, the 
company’s market share is well below 10% for now, and only focus on some minor 
projects, but we do expect the company to become one of the major suppliers for AI 
ASIC projects in coming years, considering the company’s more diversified production 
sites (mainland China + Taiwan + Thailand), while most of the AI ASIC customers are 
asking for non-Greater China capacity now.  
For the optical transceiver business, we expect ZDT to see strong growth (50%+ 
2025-29E CAGR), driven mainly by the ~50% 2026-28E volume demand CAGR and the 
company’s proactive expansion in capacity in coming years (5x more capacity over the 
next two years). Moreover, ZDT currently holds an industry-leading yield of 90%+ for its 
optical module mSAP products (vs. peers only at 60-80% per our industry checks).  
For its ABF substrate business, we believe the company will continue to have the ability 
to increase ABF price, supported by full UTR and strong demand, while the industry in 
our view remains conservative on its capacity expansion plans over the next 1-2 years 
relative to our forecasts. We expect ABF substrate LTA/Spot prices to increase by 
10-15%/20%+ QoQ per quarter from 3Q26 through at least the end of 2027, with 
shortages likely extending into 2028 (see here). Overall, the business condition for ABF 
substrate is improving with price increase and we expect the business, which was loss 
making in 2025, to expand its GM to 40%+/50%+ by end of 2026/27. 
Overall, we reiterate our Buy rating on ZDT, with a new 12m TP of NT$1,070 (from 
NT$1,025).

Earnings revisions 
We revise up our 2026/27/28/29E net income estimate by 6/21/42/49% after seeing 
the solid GM expansion from these high-growth sectors, which we believe will continue 
to be the net income driver in the coming years. We believe the high growth sectors (AI 
PCB, substrates, optical module mSAP) will account for 50%+ of total revenue in 2028 
and further expand to 60%+ in 2029, with a much higher than company average GM 
(50%+ in average in 2027-28), thus we revise up our 2027/28/29E GM by 
5.4/10.7/12.4ppt vs. previous estimate.

### Page 4

Valuation 
We revise up ZDT 12m TP from NT$1,025 to NT$1,070, still based on our SOTP based 
valuation methodology of (1) 3.5x blended P/B for ABF & BT substrate (in-line with 
upcycle industry average), (2) 15x P/E for high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in 
line with industry average), and (3) 8x P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB 
(in-line with industry downcycle average valuation), and maintain our valuation period at 
4Q26-3Q27, factoring in our earnings revision.

Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China, and has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market 
compared to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% 
ABF substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the 
most from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) 
due to the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. 
We also expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to 
drive ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We 
see further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due 
to our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.

average valuation).

content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate

ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market.

More details on SBC — SBC's 9M26 sales declined by a single digit y/y with stable 
GPM and improving opex. Pre-tax profits rose 40% y/y in 9M26. Yet the higher 
effective tax rate dragged profitability, leading to a  net loss of US$25mn in 9M26. 
Mgmt expects SBC to launch more new products in 2027E which should further 
drive the top-line and earnings recovery.  
Implications — We expect a negative share price reaction to the results print given 
the weaker-than-expected core biz and the SBC loss. Mgmt expects FY26 sales to 
fall 14-15% y/y with 4Q OEM sales seasonally softer than 3Q. New models are due 
from Jan-27 and mgmt expects low-teens growth y/y for FY27E. We will look for 
more detail on the drivers of the expected sales recovery at its 3Q26 results briefing 
next Tuesday, Nov 17.

Merida (9914.TW)

3
Merida
Valuation
Our target price for Merida shares of NT$93 is based on a 2H26-1H27E P/E of 15.5x, a 30% discount to its historical average of
22x during pre-Covid years (2014-2019), in view of soft order momentum as a result of slow recovery in bike demand, a volatile
margin trend, and low visibility of SBC.
Risks
Downside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade below our target price include: 1) e-bike adoption slower than

expected; 2) weaker restocking demand; 3) lukewarm sell-through at SBC; and 4) a weaker macro.
Upside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade above our target price include: 1) e-bike adoption faster than expected;
2) stronger restocking demand; 3) stronger sell-through at SBC; and 4) a strong macro.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

### Page 6

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 164: 261111_ml_kinik

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_ml_kinik.pdf

Date: 2026-11-11

### Page 1

Kinik

Strong advanced nodes and packaging 
expansion support decent earnings

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,050 TWD | Price: 737.00 TWD

3Q26 margin beat on favorable product mix  
Kinik’s 3Q26 sales grew 8% QoQ, mainly driven by solid diamond disk demand from N2 
and advanced packaging capacity ramp and increasing shipments of high-end 
test/reclaim wafer, more than offsetting weakness at Thailand ABU subsidiary. 3Q26 
GMs improved by 220bps QoQ to 42.2% on improving product mix across ABU and DBU 
segments, taking 3Q26 EPS to NT$3.91(vs. BofAe/Street NT$4.09/NT$4.08).  
4Q26 sales grow on continued advanced nodes expansion 
The company guided 4Q26 sales to grow slightly QoQ (BofAe + 2% QoQ), implying full-
year revenue growth of +25% YoY, backed by strength across diamond disk, test wafer, 
and reclaim wafer amid ongoing advanced nodes capacity build-out. Strong contribution 
from high-margin DBU segment, coupled with a favorable mix shift in ABU driven by 
ABF diamond wheels, should lift 4Q26 GMs further to 43.5%, in our view.  
Solid DBU demand and SBU ramp underpin '27-28  
On accelerated 3nm, 2nm and A16 capacity ramp at full utilization, alongside benign 
mature node supply/demand landscape and steady engagement with Intel on 18A/14A, 
we forecast Kinik’s diamond disk shipment to +48% CAGR over '26-28. Meanwhile, 
Kinik’s SBU sales could deliver +22% CAGR over '26-28 as 10% price hike on selected 
products take effect from early 2027 and carrier wafer demand from Micron HBM grow 
more meaningfully from 2H27. Beyond its existing business, grinding wheels for 
advanced packaging are currently under customer qualification, and would provide 
upside potential with TAM much larger than diamond disk.  
Diverse drivers and margin expansion warrant re-rating 
We keep our 2026/27/28 EPS largely unchanged at NT$14.98/26.23/36.84. Reiterate our 
Buy rating and PO of NT$1,050, still based on 32x 2H27-1H28E P/E (vs. its historical 
range 9-42x), supported by robust sales/earnings CAGR of +32%/+57% over '26-28.

Company Sector 
Semiconductor Capital Equipment

Company Description 
Kinik was established in 1953 as a conventional grinding 
wheels supplier. Since 1990-2000s, the company has 
transformed itself into a semiconductor consumables 
supplier including CMP pad conditioners (diamond disks) 
and reclaim/test wafers.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Kinik. We acknowledge its 
technology for CMP diamond disk is solid to support its 
gain in TSMC advanced nodes and Intel along with growing 
reclaimed and testing wafer demand. We expect to see 
margins expansion from mix upgrade in coming years.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Kinik (KIKCF) 
Our Price Objective of NT$1,050 is based on 32x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at upper 
half of the company's historical trading range of 9-42x. In our view, the valuation 
multiple is underpinned by 
1) around +57% earnings CAGR in 2026-28E supported by +32% sales CAGR growth and 
GMs/OpMs expansion, 2) ROE expansion to around 30% in 2025-28E, 4) net cash of 
around NT$30/share, and 5) free cash flow generation of around NT$50/share per 
annum across 2025-27E.

Downside risks to our estimates and expectations are 
1) weaker market demand, especially in the cloud AI investment moderation, leading to 
lower utilization across its major foundry and memory customers and undermining the 
company's revenue growth and operating leverage 
2) slower manufacturing technology migration by its foundry and memory customers, 
dragging the product upgrade cycle for Kinik and potentially spurring more competition 
from its peers. 
3) failure to execute or delay of technology roadmap, potentially leading to an order shift 
to its competitors.

### Page 1

ab 11 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Nan Ya Plastics
Glass fabric capacity expansion to strengthen
vertical integration
What's new
NYP announced plans to invest NT$11.5bn to add 102mn meters of glass fabric capacity
(+17% vs. existing 590mn meters capacity) in Zhangbin, Taiwan, by end-2029.
According to management, the expansion aims to meet growing demand from AI
servers, networking equipment and increasingly complex PCBs, with 60–70% of the

additional capacity allocated for internal use. Capacity will flexibly serve conventional E-
glass and AI-grade specialty glass fabric, with specialty products targeted to reach ~40%
of glass fabric revenue in 2-3 years. NYP is also expanding upstream yarn production and
adding over 500 weaving looms to support its planned glass fabric capacity expansion
by end-2029.
Implications
NYP: Glass fabric expansion to strengthen vertical integration; 1.5–2.0% EM OP

upside from 2030
We view the expansion positively, as glass fabric remains the most supply-constrained
product across NYP's CCL value chain, with the highest OPM of c40% in 3Q26 for glass
fabric. We expect further margin improvement in 4Q26, driven by continued price hikes
and a higher specialty glass fabric mix amid strong AI-related demand. Over the longer
term, the expansion should strengthen NYP's vertical integration and secure upstream
glass fabric supply for downstream CCL production. Assuming full utilization and

current ASP and margins, we estimate the 17% capacity expansion could lift EM OP by
1.5–2.0% from 2030 onwards, with further upside potential from specialty product mix
upgrades.
E-glass fabric: limited impact on external supply
NYP's planned capacity expansion of 102mn meters per year is equivalent to ~2% of
total industry capacity according to our estimates. We expect limited impact on external
E-glass supply, as NYP plans to use 60-70% of output internally, leaving only ~30-40mn

meters annually for external sales before accounting for specialty fabric allocation.
AI-grade specialty fabric: supply constraints likely to persist
We expect supply tightness to persist amid specialty yarn constraints and qualified fabric
production ramp-up. NYP's undisclosed capacity mix further limits visibility on
incremental AI-grade fabric supply.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and PT at NT$390/share
NYP is trading at 10x 27E P/E and 3.5x 27E P/B on our estimates.  Our 26/27E dividend

yields are 3.8%/6.3% at the current share price. We expect limited near-term valuation
impact from the glass fabric expansion, given its expected completion by end-2029.

Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

◼ 產品組合有望優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 21.18 元：CPC 及 VPC
稼動率皆維持高檔，預估 4Q26 營收 63.95 億元(+66.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，受惠產品組合持續優化，預估毛利率季增至 61.92%，隨營收
規模持續擴大，費用率有望年減至 25%，營益率 37.12%，稅後淨
利 20.75 億元(+119%YoY)，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 21.18 元。 
◼ 產能擴充計畫持續推進，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 68.44 元：展望後
市，隨 AI HPC 需求持續成長，探針卡朝高頻高速、大電流及高針腳
數方向發展，帶動高階測試需求維持強勁。為解決產能瓶頸，旺矽
將積極擴充產能，2026/2027 年底 MEMS 月產能為 350 萬/600 萬
針，VPC 月產能為 200 萬/250 萬針，且 PCB 廠房預計 2H27 投產，
有助提升 PCB 自製率。整體而言，預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 
68.44/118.84 元，且 CPO 相關設備有望於 2028 年較明顯挹注營
收，預估 2028 年稅後 EPS 204.56 元。 
◼ 維持買進評等，目標價 6,500 元：評價方面，以 2028 年 EPS 估算，
目前 PE 約為 21X，低於旺矽近 2 年 30-55X，考量 AI 高階測試需求
持續強勁，且 CPO 布局有望挹注中長期成長動能，長線維持買進評
等，目標價調整為 6,500 元(2028F PE 32X)。

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓 (02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號         Page 2 of 6 
旺矽為全球前五大探針卡廠商  旺矽 1995 年 7 月於新竹成立，實收資本額 9.8 億元，截至 Apr-26，員工人數
共 2,110 人。主要業務包含晶圓探針卡測試及半導體設備銷售。旺矽提供一站
式購足服務，探針卡業務包含懸臂式探針卡(Cantilever Probe Cards)、垂直式
探針卡(Vertical Probe Cards)及微機電探針卡(MEMS)等；半導體設備則例如
光電自動化設備(PA)、溫控測試設備(Thermal)、先進半導體測試設備
(Advanced Semiconductor Test, AST)等。以 3Q26 產品別營收結構而言，探
針卡 72.7%，設備 25.4%，其他 1.9%。

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓 (02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號         Page 3 of 6 
產品組合有望優化，預估 4Q26
稅後 EPS 21.18 元 
 由於 AI HPC 客 戶 拉 貨 動 能 持 續 強 勁 ， 旺 矽 Oct-26 營收 22.32 億元
(+8.98%MoM，+92.73%YoY)，優於預期。考量 CPC 及 VPC 稼動率皆維持高
檔，預估 4Q26 營收 63.95 億元(+8.3%QoQ，+66.7%YoY)。獲利方面，受惠
產品組合持續優化，預估毛利率季增至 61.92%，隨營收規模持續擴大，費用
率有望年減至 25%，營益率 37.12%，稅後淨利 20.75 億元(+10.4%QoQ，
+119%YoY)，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 21.18 元。

產能擴充計畫持續推進，預 估
2026 年稅後 EPS 68.44 元

展望後市，隨 AI 與 HPC 需求持續成長，探針卡朝高頻高速、大電流及高針腳
數方向發展，帶動高階測試需求維持強勁。為解決產能瓶頸，旺矽將積極擴充
產能，包含：MEMS 月產能自 2025 年底 100 萬針，預期 2026 年底提升至 350
萬針，2027 年底目標為 600 萬針；VPC 月產能則自 2025 年底 120 萬針，預
期 2026 年底提升為 200 萬針，2027 年底有望再增加至 250 萬針；CPC 月產
能則維持持平略增。廠房方面，旺矽 Nov-26 中旬公告投入 22 億元進行湖口廠
機電工程，PCB 廠房預計如期於 2H27 投產，有助提升 PCB 自製率；另，
CPO 設備所需廠房改建，預計 1Q27 完成。資本支出方面，預估 2026/2027 年
分別為 15-18 億元及 20 億元，2027 年主要用於設備採購。

整體而言，投顧預估 2026 年營收 214.7 億元(+60.5%YoY)，受惠產品組合持
續優化，毛利率 60.5%，營益率 36%，稅後淨利 67.1 億元(+111.1%YoY)，上
修 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 68.44 元(原預估 64.97 元)。

Exhibit 6：旺矽擴產時程推估

Sources: Company，Taishin 
高階測試需求續強，預估 2027
年稅後 EPS 118.84 元 
 旺矽自 2026 年起整併 LED 與 AST 部門，並持續布局 CPO 檢測專案，目前主
要聚焦於 CPO insertion 2 之 EIC/PIC 測試及 insertion 3 之 die-level 測試應
用。由於 CPO 測試涉及光電整合，技術門檻及客製化程度較高，目前仍持續
與客戶進行驗證及開發。其中，insertion 2 既有客戶預計 2026 年底更新驗證
進度，另有新客戶持續接洽中，相關進展有望於 1H27 較為明朗；insertion 3
則已有多家國際客戶出貨工程機，且持續交機中，有望 1H27 逐步轉為量產機
需求。展望後市，隨 AI 高速傳輸需求提升，CPO 相關測試設備有望成為 AST
業務中長期成長動能，惟考量設備交期較長及客戶驗收認證所需時間，預期
2028 年營收貢獻將較為顯著，CPO 相關業務營收占比有望達雙位數。

展望 2027 年，隨新增產能逐步開出及新廠投入營運，且客戶組成較為多元，

 저점을 받쳐줄 실적

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 500,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

## Document 289: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지불리한 환경에_79fc85e4

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지불리한 환경에_79fc85e4.pdf

 절대 1.9  2.4  (15.2)  
 상대 24.4  20.4  (43.5)  
 절대 (달러환산) 6.1  13.8  (8.6)

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

## Document 348: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

## Document 375: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

## Document 430: RBAは２回連続の利上げ決定も、景気減速懸念で追加利上げには慎重姿勢 ～インフレ圧力の根強さが利上げを促すも、景気下振れ懸念_d8ef4396

Source PDF: all report pdf/RBAは２回連続の利上げ決定も、景気減速懸念で追加利上げには慎重姿勢 ～インフレ圧力の根強さが利上げを促すも、景気下振れ懸念_d8ef4396.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 4 日

1/6 
RBA は２回連続の利上げ決定も、景気減速懸念で追加利上げには慎重姿勢 
～インフレ圧力の根強さが利上げを促すも、景気下振れ懸念や追加利上げ期待の後退が豪ドルの重しに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ オーストラリア準備銀行（ＲＢＡ）は、11 月２～３日に開催した定例会合において、政策金
利を 25bp 引き上げて 4.85％とすることを決定した。利上げは２会合連続、年明け以降５回
目となり、政策金利は18 年ぶりの高水準となった。 
○ 背景には、原油高に加え、国内の需給ひっ迫を反映してインフレ圧力が根強く、物価上昇が
幅広い財・サービスに波及していることがある。ＲＢＡは物価見通しを大幅に上方修正し、
インフレ率が目標レンジに収束する時期も従来の想定から後ずれすると見込んでいる。 
○ 一方で、今回の利上げ決定においては、理事９人のうち３人が据え置きを支持するなど、景
気減速への警戒感も強まっている。ブロック総裁は、これまでの利上げによる累積的な影響
を見極める必要性を強調しており、ＲＢＡは当面、政策金利を現行水準に据え置き、物価や
雇用、不動産市場の動向を慎重に見極める展開が予想される。ただし、インフレ圧力が高止
まりすれば追加利上げに動く可能性も残ると考えられる。 
○ 豪ドル相場については、利上げが事前に織り込まれていたことに加え、追加利上げ期待の後
退を受けて弱含んだ。先行きについても、国内景気の減速懸念や米国の金融政策、中国景気、
中東情勢などの影響を受けつつ、当面は上値の重い展開が続く可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/6 
【RBAは２会合連続の利上げ決定、年明け以降５回目、政策金利は 18 年ぶりの高水準に】 
オーストラリア準備銀行（ＲＢＡ）は、11 月２～３日の日程で開催した定例の金融政策委員会に
おいて、政策金利であるオフィシャルキ
ャッシュレート（ＯＣＲ）を 25bp 引き上
げて 4.85％とすることを決定した（図
１）。ＲＢＡは、２月の定例会合で２年３
ヶ月ぶりの利上げに動き、５月の定例会
合まで３会合連続の利上げを実施した。
その後、６月会合、８月会合では金利を
据え置く様子見姿勢に転じたものの、９
月会合で３会合ぶりの利上げに動いた。
このため、今回は２会合連続、年明け以
降５回目となる。今回の利上げにより、
ＯＣＲは18 年ぶりの水準となった。 
事前の市場予想では、ＲＢＡが利上げに動くとの見方が大宗を占めた。背景には、ＲＢＡ高官か
ら先行きの政策運営について、タカ派姿勢を維持する発言が相次いだことがある。なかでもハウザ
ー副総裁は、原油高などを理由にインフレ見通しが「著しく悪化している」として、金融政策では
供給ショックに対処できないとしつつ、コスト上昇がインフレ期待の上昇に波及することを防ぐ必
要があるとの考えを示した。そのうえ、物価動向がＲＢＡの想定を上回る可能性が高いとの見方を
示すなど、インフレに対する警戒感を強調した。同氏の発言は政策運営の方向性を明示したもので
はないものの、インフレ見通しの悪化に加え、インフレ期待を抑え込む必要性を強調したことは、
金融市場に追加利上げを意識させるに十分であったと考えられる（注１）。

注１ 10 月21 日付レポート「弱含む動きをみせてきた豪ドル相場は持ち直しに向かうか」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666763.html）

3/6 
７-９月のインフレ率は前年同期比＋3.9％と前期（同＋3.9％）から横ばいで推移し、ＲＢＡが定
める目標レンジ（２～３％）を引き続き上回るとともに、ＲＢＡが８月会合で示した見通し（前年
比＋3.6％）をも上回った（図２）。前期
比も＋1.3％と前期（同＋0.6％）から上
昇ペースが加速し、食料品やエネルギー
など生活必需品の物価上昇に加え、サー
ビス物価も上昇基調を強める動きが確
認された。コアインフレ率（トリム平均
値）も前年同期比＋3.7％と前期（同＋
3.6％）からわずかに伸びが加速してお
り、ＲＢＡの見通し（同＋3.3％）も上回
っ た。前期 比も＋1.0％ と前期（ 同＋
0.8％）から上昇ペースが加速しており、
基調的なインフレ圧力が強まっていることがうかがえた。さらに、ＲＢＡのブロック総裁が議会証
言において、これまでの利上げにより物価が目標に回帰させることが可能か、今回会合で議論する
考えを示すとともに、物価上昇圧力が依然強いとの認識を示した。このため、金融市場では利上げ
が実施されるとの見方が一段と強まった（注２）。

【評決は６対３に割れる、インフレ見通しを上方修正する一方、景気の下振れリスクにも警戒】 
会合後に公表された声明文では、理事９人のうち６人が利上げに賛成した一方、３人は据え置き
を支持するなど、金融引き締めの必要性を巡って判断が分かれたことが明らかにされた。前回の９
月会合では、全会一致で利上げを決定したことを踏まえれば、今回の票割れは、インフレ抑制を優
先する姿勢が維持される一方、景気減速への警戒が理事会内で強まりつつあることを示唆している。 
物価動向について「依然高水準にある」、「足元のデータはインフレの広がりを示唆している」と
の認識を示した。加えて、「インフレ見通しも大幅に上方修正された」として、その理由に「イラン
情勢の悪化による燃料やその他商品市況の上昇を一部反映した」ことを挙げた。一方で、「同国内に
おける供給能力のひっ迫も想定以上にやや厳しい状況にある」との見方を示し、「 ＡＩ（人工知能）
関連投資が技術関連のインフレ圧力を招いているほか、多くの財・サービスでインフレ圧力が依然
高い」、「多くの企業がコスト上昇圧力に直面するなか、財・サービス価格をすでに引き上げ、ない
し検討を行っており、インフレ率は 2027 年を通じて高止まりすると予想している」とした。その
結果、「インフレ率が目標レンジに達するのは2027 年後半と従来の想定から約３四半期遅れる」と
して、「インフレ期待への上振れリスクをもたらしている」との見方を示した。 
会合にあわせて公表した最新の『四半期金融政策報告』では、物価見通しが大幅に上方修正され

注２ 10 月28 日付レポート「豪ドル相場に持ち直しの兆し、RBA による追加利上げ観測が再燃」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666938.html）

### Page 4

4/6 
た。2026 年末のインフレ率は前年同期比＋4.5％と、８月会合時点の見通し（同＋3.6％）から大幅
に上方修正された。コアインフレ率についても、2027 年半ばの見通しを前年同期比＋3.5％と、８
月会合時点（同＋3.0％）から上方修正した。そのうえで、ＲＢＡはインフレ率が目標レンジの上限
である３％以下となる時期を 2027 年末近くまで後ずれすると見込んでおり、目標レンジの中央値
である 2.5％への収束も2028 年半ばになるとの見方を示している。加えて、原油高などに伴う企業
のコスト増加が販売価格や賃金設定に波及し、家計や企業のインフレ期待が高止まりするリスクに
も警戒感を示した。 
一方、金融環境については「年明け以降の利上げにより引き締まった」との見方を示した。その
うえで、「経済は減速し、不動産価格も大幅に下落し、失業率も上昇している」として、先行きは
「向こう１年で生産の伸びは一段と鈍化が見込まれ、生産能力への圧力が緩和され、インフレ率を
目標レンジに戻す一助になることが期待される」とした。また、「金利上昇や不動産価格・取引高の
下落は、不動産投資や家計消費を下押しする」とする一方、「データセンター関連投資を含む、企業
部門による設備投資は当面堅調な推移が見込まれ」、「雇用も増加が見込まれるが、失業率は８月会
合時点の想定より大幅に上昇する」との見通しを示した。 
ただし、実体経済や物価見通しについては「依然として不確実性が高い」として、「イラン情勢の
行方は見通せず、インフレ率が想定を上回り、実体経済が想定を下回るシナリオも考えられる」と
し、「 不確実性が長期化すれば、世界経済や同国の経済成長率が低下する可能性がある」とした。一
方で、「足元ではＡＩ関連投資による押し上げ効果がイラン情勢の悪影響を上回っており、主要貿
易相手国の景気は想定を上回る力強さが続いている」ものの、「同国では生産性の伸びの鈍化が潜
在成長率の重しとなり、不動産市場の低迷の度合いや実体経済への影響にも不確実性が残る」との
見方を示した。 
今回の決定については「インフレ期待が高止まりするリスクが高まっている」ほか、「インフレ見
通しや上振れリスクを踏まえ、目標レンジに戻すためにはさらなる引き締めが妥当と判断した」と
した。そのうえで、先行きも「インフレ率を持続的に目標レンジに戻すために必要な措置を引き続
き講じる」として、「経済指標や見通し、リスクに関する評価を注視する」としつつ、「政策対応は
情勢変化に対応できる態勢が整っており、物価安定と完全雇用の実現というマンデートに注力する」
との考えを示した。ただし、９月会合では追加利上げに言及する文言が含まれていたものの、今回
はそうした文言は削除されるなど明確な方向感を示されなかった。

【ブロック総裁は過去の利上げ効果を見極める姿勢を強調、追加利上げには慎重な構えをみせる】 
一方で、今回の会合後に実施されたブロック総裁の記者会見では、先行きの金融政策運営につい
て慎重な姿勢がうかがわれた。同氏は、今回の利上げ決定について、原油価格の上昇による直接的
なインフレへの対応ではなく、従来からの国内における需給ひっ迫や、エネルギー価格の上昇が幅
広い物価に波及する二次的な影響を抑制することへの対応と説明した。ただし、年明け以降に実施
した５回の利上げのうち、直近の利上げの効果は実体経済に十分に浸透していないとして、今後公
表される経済指標を見極める必要性を強調した。今回の会合で据え置きを支持した理事についても、

### Page 5

5/6 
不動産市場の調整や失業率の上昇、賃金上昇率が加速していないことなどを重視し、金融引き締め
の効果を確認するために時間を置くべきだと判断したことを明らかにした。 
事実、足元の国内景気にはこれまでの金融引き締めの影響が広がりをみせている。９月の失業率
は 4.7％と上昇傾向にあるとともに、不動産価格の下落や住宅ローン需要が減少していることなど、
金利上昇に敏感な分野で調整圧力が強まる動きが確認されている。ＲＢＡは今回公表した最新の経
済見通しにおいて、2027 年末の実質 GDP 成長率を前年同期比＋1.3％と、８月会合時点の見通し（同
＋1.6％）から下方修正している。さらに、失業率も 2028 年末に 5.1％まで上昇すると予想してお
り、８月会合時点の見通し（4.8％）から上方修正している。同氏は、中心的な見通しでは景気後退
を想定していないとしつつ、経済成長率の大幅な鈍化や家計消費の弱さが続くとの認識を示した。 
注目されるのは、前述した通りＲＢＡが声明文から、必要に応じて追加利上げを行うとの明示的
な表現を削除したことである。同氏は、これを金融政策の方向転換を示すものと受け止めるべきで
はないと説明しており、追加利上げの可能性を排除してはいない。ただし、理事会内において判断
が分かれたことに加え、これまでの利上げによる累積的な影響を考慮すれば、ＲＢＡが今後も断続
的に利上げを実施する可能性は低下したと考えられる。当面は現行水準で政策金利を据え置き、物
価や雇用、不動産市場の動向を慎重に見極める展開が予想される。一方で、原油価格の高止まりや
インフレ期待の上昇が続けば、2027 年前半に追加利上げに動く可能性も残る。反対に、不動産市場
の調整が深刻化するとともに、個人消費や雇用の悪化が想定以上に進む場合には、金融引き締めの
長期化を見直す必要性が強まるとみられる。

【豪ドル相場は利上げ決定後に弱含む動き、先行きも当面は上値の重い展開が続く可能性】 
金融市場では、前述の通り今回の利上げが事前に相当程度織り込まれていたこともあり、決定直
後の豪ドル相場は対米ドルで下落した
（図３）。声明において、追加利上げを明
示する文言が削除されたことに加え、理
事会内部において据え置きを支持する
意見がみられたことも、追加的な金融引
き締めの実施に対する市場の見方を後
退させたと考えられる。今後の豪ドル相
場を巡っては、ＲＢＡの政策金利が高水
準で維持されることが一定の下支え要
因になると見込まれるものの、国内景気
の減速に伴って追加利上げ期待が後退
すれば、豪ドル高圧力は限定的となる可能性がある。 
さらに、豪ドル相場は国内の金融政策だけでなく、米国の金融政策や中国景気、国際商品市況、
世界的な投資家のリスク選好の影響を受けやすい。仮にＲＢＡが追加利上げに踏み切る一方、米国
の金融引き締め姿勢が弱まれば、米国とオーストラリアの間の金利差の変化が豪ドル相場を押し上

### Page 6

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
6/6
げる可能性がある。しかし、米国でもインフレ圧力が根強く、金融引き締めの長期化観測が強まる
場合には、米ドル高が豪ドルの上値を抑えるとみられる。また、中国景気の減速や中東情勢の悪化
によるリスク回避姿勢の強まりも、豪ドルの下押し要因となり得る。こうした点を踏まえると、豪
ドル相場は短期的に一進一退の展開となるとともに、明確な上昇基調への転換には、豪州の景気減
速への懸念後退や米国との金融政策の方向性の違いが一段と明確になることが必要であろう。

## Document 476: Who really wins from AI_ Ask India (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Who really wins from AI_ Ask India (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 23
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Who really wins from AI?  
Ask India

1 
Authors 
Adrian Cox 
Research Analyst

November 19, 2026 
If you really want to understand the true potential for AI, look beyond Silicon 
Valley to a market that has flown under the radar of AI investors until now: 
India.  
As markets agonise over who will win the AI race, India is quietly testing whether 
it even matters. With little homegrown AI technology but a vast, young, tech-
savvy population, it may show that creating new technology counts less than 
adopting it at scale. 
This report sets out the bull and bear cases for India and AI. It also draws out 
some unexpected lessons for other markets that want the benefits of AI without 
letting jobs and industries get steamrollered in the process. 
And it looks at the short-term case for India as a diversifier from AI volatility. 
India’s low correlation with other global indices is suddenly looking like a 
strength, helping to drive record inflows of foreign investment into Indian 
equities. 
1. What would winning AI actually mean? 
2. Scoring India’s AI potential 
3. Six reasons India could be an AI winner 
4. Four ways it could go wrong 
5. Investment snapshot: short-term AI diversifier and long-term 
beneficiary 
The US and China have the models, the chips and hundreds of billions of dollars 
in data centre investments, but rapidly developing India has almost 1.5 billion 
potential users, digital rails that are already built, an enormous technology 
workforce and some of the world’s largest unmet needs in finance, healthcare 
and agriculture. 
India recently became ChatGPT’s largest market by weekly users and, thanks to 
its pervasive digital infrastructure, even a street vendor selling a single piece of 
fruit will typically hold up a QR code on their phone for instant payment. 
“Smartphone scrolling has taken over from cricket as India’s No. 1 pastime,” a 
banker told me on a recent visit to Mumbai, India’s financial capital, where 
swarms of battered three-wheeled black and yellow auto rickshaws weave 
between fleets of freshly buffed Mercedes sedans. 
The real test for AI will not be in the frontier labs of San Francisco but on 
streets like these in Mumbai and thousands of cities like it.

### Page 2

19 November 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Figure 1: Foreign institutions have invested record amounts in Indian stocks in 
November (USDbn, monthly)

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research

What would winning AI actually mean?  
Investors in the US, China and Korea have so far largely focused on AI capabilities, 
picking the winners in the AI trade in a volatile daily referendum on potential gains 
years in the future.  
By contrast, India can adopt an infrastructure-lite approach that sidesteps the 
whole debate, embracing the benefits without incurring the costs of trying to stay 
on the bleeding edge. 
It can plug off-the-shelf software into well-developed national digital systems, 
tapping into the world’s largest population, with a median age of 29, and 
deploying its considerable buying power as two blocs compete to set the global 
standard. 
Success does not need to mean building the most powerful foundation model. 
Instead, it may be deploying it for: 
1. Higher productivity 
2. Faster income growth 
3. Better financial, educational and healthcare access 
4. More productive employment rather than mass displacement 
5. Higher-value IT and service exports 
6. Capturing enough of the AI value chain domestically 
While India has fundamental economic and infrastructure challenges, it ranks No. 
3 in the world for AI vibrancy, albeit far behind the leaders US and China, 
according to the Stanford HAI Global AI Vibrancy Tool. That is thanks to its strong 
position in Research & Development and in talent.

### Page 3

19 November 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

Figure 2: India ranks No. 3 in Stanford Global AI Vibrancy Tool

Source: Stanford HAI Global AI Vibrancy Tool (2024), Deutsche Bank Research

And the early evidence suggests that India may lead the way in adopting AI. 
It ranks No 1 in AI trust and regular intentional use at work, according to data from 
the University of Melbourne and KPMG. Only Nigeria, Egypt and China come 
close. By comparison, in most North American and European countries, only 
about half of employees report using AI tools regularly for work and trust is even 
lower. 
Figure 3: India leads in AI trust and intentional use at work

Source: University of Melbourne, KPMG International, Stanford HAI AI Index 2026, Deutsche Bank Research
